- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-representation**: Use when you have raw mass spectrometry imaging data (2D or 3D spatial coordinates with full mass-to-charge spectra) and want to train a probabilistic deep learning classifier for tumor delineation or tissue classification. - **mass-spectrometry-instrument-resolution-modeling**: Use when when correcting LC-MS fractional abundances of measured isotopologues (FAM) from isotope labeling experiments where instrument resolution (e.
